# README and wiki claim check against the code

Repository `Alistra-za/Ssedds`, branch `main` at `d5bd603` (after "docs: humanize pass over README and wiki"). Every factual claim in README.md and the 13 pages under docs/wiki was checked against code only: crates/, presets/, schema/, Cargo.toml, rust-toolchain.toml and the licence files. Nothing under docs/ was used as evidence and no doc was edited.

Verdicts. **TRUE**: a cited code location shows exactly this. **FALSE**: the code does something else, or the named thing does not exist. **PARTLY**: the gist holds but a stated detail (default, name, list, threshold, place) is wrong. **UNVERIFIABLE**: a benchmark, research, history or external-tool statement with no footprint in code; not judged either way. **N/A**: a bookkeeping row (for example the "undocumented flags" check).

## Read this first

The docs are in better shape than feared. Of about 1,480 atomic claims, 5 are false and 68 are partly wrong; the rest match the code or cannot be judged from code. Nothing contradicts a later ruling on formats, tags, alpha, mips, presets or NVTT. The errors cluster in the GUI guide (layout and widget details) and in wiki prose that over-generalises a rule.

The five FALSE claims:

1. **GUI-Guide.md:54** says the Sets column is on the right. The layout is Textures, Sets, then the centre; the only right-hand panel is the optional 3D set view. README.md:71 repeats the same order (textures, settings, sets).
2. **GUI-Guide.md:92** says edited tiles are marked until you save. Tiles carry no mark; only the window title gets a dot.
3. **GUI-Guide.md:198** says the drag-and-drop flows were checked by code review only. `crates/gui/tests/sets_model.rs` has 19 automated drop tests.
4. **Texture-Types.md:286** says you can pick `complex_grass` from the GUI sub-type picker. The picker never offers it; it comes from the set shader, the GRAS auto-tag, or `subtype` in `[files]`.
5. **CLI-Reference.md:117** says that with `--sidecar` a missing sidecar triggers a re-encode. It is rewritten from the manifest record instead; only a failed write re-encodes.

PARTLY rows worth fixing because they would change what a user does:

- **Fonts** (Installation.md:13, Troubleshooting.md:67, GUI-Guide.md:327): only Segoe UI is required; Segoe UI Symbol, Segoe UI Emoji and Consolas are optional.
- **`--fill` precedence** (Project-Config.md:86): it sits with unscoped `--set`, below `subtype:` and `file:` scoped sets, not above them.
- **`--accept-suggestions high`** (CLI-Reference.md:32 area, row CLI-25): gates sub-type suggestions only; high-confidence alpha suggestions apply regardless.
- **Range errors** (Project-Config.md:7): most semantic range errors are per-file CFG-INVALID, not an up-front exit 2.
- **`subtype` inside `[subtypes.*]` or `[defaults]`** (Project-Config.md:109, 198): accepted and silently overwritten, not rejected.
- **FMT-BC5-NORMAL** (Troubleshooting.md:159; README.md:112 undersells it the same way): also fires for R8 and RG8, not only BC4/BC5.
- **`--mem-budget` error text** (Troubleshooting.md:88): a bad size prints `not a valid size`, not the quoted message.
- **`sdc validate` scope** (Validation-and-Output.md:46): cannot run the mip-error, exact-opaque, coverage or BC6H-peak checks on a foreign file.
- **GRAS auto-tag** (README.md:84, Mod-Scanning.md:9 area): a GUI-only high-confidence suggestion, only under non-vanilla targets, only for 1:2 files passing the shape tests.
- **Legacy FourCC headers** (README.md:19): under `header = auto` BC4/BC5 get DX10, not the `BC4U`/`BC5U` legacy form.
- **rgbcx "level 18 for BC1, BC3, BC4 and BC5"** (README.md:11): level 18 applies to BC1 and the BC3 colour block; BC4/BC5 take a search radius.
- **`sdc encoders` path** (Installation.md:120, FAQ): printed with forward slashes.
- **Files the GUI writes** (FAQ row SCAN-126): list omits the shader cache under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\sdc`, `scan.lock`, NVTT temp folders and `gui.toml.bad`.

One naming observation outside the tables: the README and wiki call the project **Tessalith**, but that name has no footprint in code. Binaries are `sdc` and `sdc-gui`, window titles read `sdc — <folder>` and `TruePBR JSON — sdc`, and the user folders are `%APPDATA%\sdc` and `%LOCALAPPDATA%\sdc`. Not wrong, but a reader will not find "Tessalith" anywhere the program shows itself.

## Every FALSE and PARTLY row

FALSE first, then PARTLY, in page order. Row ids point into the per-page tables below.

| Row | Doc location | Claim | Verdict | What the code actually does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLI-79 | CLI-Reference.md:117 | With `--sidecar`, a missing sidecar also triggers a re-encode | FALSE | A missing sidecar on an up-to-date output is written from the manifest record and the file stays up to date. Only if that write fails does the file re-encode. |
| GUI-23 | GUI-Guide.md:54 | Sets column is on the **right** | FALSE | Both the Textures and the Sets columns are left panels, drawn in that order (Textures, then Sets), with the centre to their right. The right edge holds the Advanced drawer (ui/advanced.rs:35, 52 `Panel::right`) and the 3D set-preview panel (ui/mod.rs:105 `Panel::right("set-3d")`). |
| GUI-65 | GUI-Guide.md:92 | Edited texture tiles are marked until you save | FALSE | A tile carries no "edited"/unsaved mark; the only unsaved-edit indicator is the `●` in the window title (ui/header.rs:23-25). |
| GUI-126 | GUI-Guide.md:198 | The drag-and-drop flows were checked by code review only, not by automated tests | FALSE | The model-level drop flows (`drop_on_set`, `drop_new_set`, replace/choice outcomes) have automated tests (29 `drop_on_set`/`drop_new_set` calls across crates/gui/tests). |
| TEX-150 | TT:286 | Pick `complex_grass` as the sub-type in the GUI under *Packed and data* | FALSE | The sub-type pickers never offer `complex_grass`; it is chosen by the set shader or the GRAS auto-tag (or `subtype` in `[files]`). |
| RF-19 | README.md:22 | …at level 18 | PARTLY | Level 18 applies to BC1 and the BC3 colour block only; BC4/BC5 (and the BC3 alpha block) are driven by `bc45_search_radius`, not a level. |
| RF-33 | README.md:26 | DX10 where the format needs it, legacy FourCC where it can | PARTLY | Under the default `header = auto` BC4 and BC5 get DX10 headers even though a legacy FourCC form exists (`BC4U`/`BC5U`); legacy is only used automatically for BC1, BC3 and RGBA8 UNORM. An explicit `header = legacy` can select it. |
| RF-103 | README.md:71 | GUI: three columns — textures, settings of the selection, material sets | PARTLY | Orchestrator check of crates/gui/src/ui/mod.rs:71-112: the columns run Textures, Sets, then the centre (settings or preview); the only right-hand panel is the optional 3D set view. The README order "textures, settings, sets" puts Sets on the right, the same slip as GUI-Guide.md:54. |
| RF-131 | README.md:82 | Grass textures used by GRAS records are tagged as complex grass | PARTLY | It is a high-confidence suggestion, only for GRAS-referenced files that are 1:2 and pass the corner/unit-share tests, and only in the GUI; the CLI never auto-tags. |
| RF-146 | README.md:150 | Windows 10 or 11, x64 | PARTLY | x64 Windows is the only target; the "10 or 11" minimum has no code footprint. |
| RF-149 | README.md:152 | Visual Studio 2022 Build Tools with the C++ workload (MSVC) | PARTLY | MSVC is required by the target and the `cc` build; the specific "2022" version has no code footprint. |
| RF-182 | README.md:211 | Credits for AlphaBloom's Auto Coverage, Ignacio Castaño's coverage-preserving mips and jiayev's TruePBR-Manager are in docs/CREDITS.md | PARTLY | The three influences have code footprints; whether docs/CREDITS.md actually lists them cannot be confirmed under the evidence rules. "jiayev" appears nowhere in crates/. |
| RR-30 | README.md:112 | BC5 is the default only for `_flow` | PARTLY | `generic_two_channel` also defaults to BC5. It has no suffix (never produced by suggest.rs:68-140), so it is user-selected only, but "only for `_flow`" is not literally true. |
| CLI-25 | CLI-Reference.md:32 | `high` accepts every High-confidence sub-type and alpha suggestion | PARTLY | High-confidence sub-type suggestions are gated by the flag. High-confidence *alpha* suggestions are applied whether or not `--accept-suggestions high` is given; the flag does not control them. |
| CLI-74 | CLI-Reference.md:117 | ... or NVTT version differs | PARTLY | Only outputs whose profile uses `encoder.backend = "nvtt"` compare the NVTT version; built-in-encoder outputs ignore it. |
| CLI-86 | CLI-Reference.md:123-133 | "Each line on stderr has one of these shapes" (list of 7 shapes) | PARTLY | The list omits two shapes the code also prints: `error <input>: <CODE> <message>` and `info  <input>: CFG-FILTER-IGNORED <message>` (padded to 5). |
| CLI-93 | CLI-Reference.md:143 | skipped = unresolved, blocked, duplicate, DDS input, existing output with no record, `--on-existing skip` | PARTLY | The list omits `PACK-CONFLICT` (a pre-packed file replaced under `packing.prefer = "raw"`), which is also counted in `skipped`. Packing inputs and RNM layers are correctly *not* counted (api.rs:1585-1623 emit without `skip`). |
| CLI-109 | CLI-Reference.md:191-199 | DDS input prints path, `format <F> <srgb\|unorm>  WxH  N mips  <kind> header  cubemap <b>`, then per-mip alpha lines | PARTLY | Line shapes match, but the header kind prints as `Dx10` / `Legacy` (serde default of the variant name), not `dx10` as the example shows. |
| CLI-117 | CLI-Reference.md:223 | Skipped and failed files are listed as `convert` would report them | PARTLY | The same files are reported, but in `inspect`'s own two-line format (`<path>` then `  skipped ...` / `  cannot be read: ...`), not convert's `skip  ...` / `FAIL  ...` lines. |
| CLI-122 | CLI-Reference.md:238 | `--subtype <id>` default inferred from the file name | PARTLY | Inference uses the file's suffix, its game path (e.g. `textures/pbr/`, `interface/`) and, for formats with alpha, the decoded mip-0 alpha, not only the name. Minor. |
| CLI-125 | CLI-Reference.md:239 | Inferred as `remove` when every mip-0 alpha >= 254, otherwise the sub-type's default | PARTLY | Missing case: when the file stores alpha (min < 254) and the sub-type's default is `remove`, the policy is inferred as `data`, not the sub-type's default. |
| CLI-145 | CLI-Reference.md:310 | Fixes: mip count, flags, caps, legacy/DX10 header form, `_SRGB` tag | PARTLY | Those five are fixed, but so are linear size/pitch, reserved fields, a non-standard DDPF_NORMAL flag, DX10 dimension/array/misc fields and depth/pixel-format size; the list is incomplete. |
| CLI-195 | CLI-Reference.md:418 | `validate --json` record: `kind` = `dds_validation`, `path`, `readable`, `subtype`, `alpha_policy`, `judged`, `note`, `passed`, `summary`, `warnings` | PARTLY | The listed fields exist but the record also carries `schema` (1). An unreadable file gets a different record (`readable: false`, `passed: false`, `error`) without subtype/alpha_policy/judged/note/summary/warnings. |
| GUI-32 | GUI-Guide.md:38 | Only sub-types the current Mode allows are offered | PARTLY | The dropdown also keeps the texture's current sub-type even when the Mode does not offer it. |
| GUI-53 | GUI-Guide.md:66 | "What kind of texture is this?": Sub-type **buttons** and **Confirm** | PARTLY | It is a sub-type dropdown (ComboBox) plus a **Confirm** button, not buttons per sub-type. |
| GUI-68 | GUI-Guide.md:93 | Without unsaved edits, the GUI reloads it when the window regains focus or when you save or export | PARTLY | Reload happens on focus and on Export; Save does not check the disk (and a clean document is not even written). |
| GUI-98 | GUI-Guide.md:163 | Toolbar: channel isolation (RGBA and alpha mask) | PARTLY | The channel buttons are RGBA, R, G, B, A and α-mask (six), not only RGBA and alpha mask. |
| GUI-107 | GUI-Guide.md:175 | Bottom drawer with a *Files* tab (queued, running, done, up to date, skipped, failed, per file) | PARTLY | Row states are `queued`, `running`, `converted`, `validation failed`, `up to date`, `skipped: …`, `failed: …`, `cancelled` — there is no "done" label, and "validation failed" and "cancelled" are missing from the list. |
| GUI-120 | GUI-Guide.md:193 | Right-click a set or member for its menu: move, share, duplicate, **Rename…**, **Delete set** | PARTLY | The set menu has no Share or Duplicate entries (those are answers of the drop dialog, sets_column.rs:662-676) and "move" is Move up / Move down; a member's right-click opens its slot menu (From this set, Path…, Clear, From another set, Lock/Unlock), not the set menu. |
| GUI-128 | GUI-Guide.md:204 | In 2D each slot is a card in PGPatcher slot order: Diffuse, Normal, Emissive, Height, Cubemap, RMAOS, Coat normal / fuzz, Subsurface | PARTLY | There is no Cubemap card (slot 5 has no `SlotKind`; cubemap exists only as a slot command, ui/slot_commands.rs:21-30). Slot 3 may be "Emissive _g" or "Skin _sk", and slot 7 is two separate cards, "Coat _cnr" and "Fuzz _f". |
| GUI-143 | GUI-Guide.md:233 | Coat settings: Coat colour, Coat specular level, Coat roughness, Coat strength, Coat diffuse, Coat normal (_cnr), Coat parallax, Inner UV scale | PARTLY | Coat colour and Inner UV scale are advanced keys: they appear under *Advanced TruePBR settings* → Coat settings, not in the main Coat settings group. |
| GUI-146 | GUI-Guide.md:236 | Advanced TruePBR settings: Path contains, Mesh path contains, Rename, UV and cubemap, Vertex colours and depth, Texture locks | PARTLY | The advanced section also holds a Coat settings group (Coat colour, Inner UV scale); "UV and cubemap" holds Smooth angle, Auto UV, UV scale and Cubemap path. |
| GUI-149 | GUI-Guide.md:242 | **Reset** returns a field, or the whole set, to its default | PARTLY | There is a per-field `reset` button; no control resets a whole set's TruePBR settings (the bulk "Reset all settings" touches `sdc.toml` only, ui/dialogs.rs:280-281). |
| GUI-156 | GUI-Guide.md:254 | Open it with **Editor…** in the header or from a set's TruePBR box; a window of its own titled "TruePBR JSON" | PARTLY | The native window's title is "TruePBR JSON — sdc"; "TruePBR JSON" is its heading. |
| GUI-169 | GUI-Guide.md:275 | Background: Studio HDRI (Poly Haven, CC0), Grey, Solid | PARTLY | The choice is Solid or Studio HDRI; "Grey" is a 0–100 % slider that sets the solid colour's level, not a third option. |
| GUI-186 | GUI-Guide.md:306 | Project-wide fills (legacy): fills in `defaults.packing.fill.*`; **Clear** removes them | PARTLY | It is a single line, not a section, and it is shown only while the file holds such fills. |
| GUI-199 | GUI-Guide.md:327 | Needs Segoe UI, Segoe UI Symbol, Segoe UI Emoji and Consolas, and refuses to start if they are missing | PARTLY | Only Segoe UI (`segoeui.ttf`) is required; Segoe UI Symbol, Segoe UI Emoji and Consolas are optional fallbacks that are skipped when absent. |
| GUI-204 | Home.md:8 | The CLI and the GUI both read and write the same `sdc.toml` | PARTLY | The CLI reads `sdc.toml`; nothing in the CLI writes it. Only the GUI writes it (crates/gui/src/project.rs:463-477). |
| CFG-6 | PC:7 | Values outside their allowed range are errors that stop the whole run with exit code 2 | PARTLY | Only type/deserialisation failures (unknown enum value, non-u8, etc.) stop the run with exit 2. Semantic ranges (`alpha.threshold` 1..=254, `mips.bake_clamp`, `rgbcx_level`, `roughness_default`, rnm strengths, swizzle) are checked per file in `check_profile` as `CFG-INVALID` (Error status for that file), not an up-front exit 2. |
| CFG-23 | PC:84 | Layer 9: accepted suggestions (`--accept-suggestions high`, or always in the GUI) | PARTLY | Suggestions decide only the sub-type, never a field value; in the sub-type decision they rank below `--set`/`[files]`, rules and a set-chosen shader, so "layer 9" is only the `Provenance` enum position (presets.rs:280), not a settings layer. The GUI always accepting (`AcceptSuggestions::High`) is true. |
| CFG-25 | PC:86 | Layer 11: `--set` unscoped, then `subtype:`, then `file:` scoped; then `--fill` | PARTLY | `--fill` is an unscoped `packing.fill.<role>` override appended after every `--set`; it sits in the unscoped group (rank 0), so a `subtype:` or `file:` scoped `--set packing.fill.*` beats it. The doc puts `--fill` above the scoped sets. |
| CFG-29 | PC:92 | A non-fitting backend is kept only if the same layer set both, or a more specific layer pinned it | PARTLY | Kept when the same patch set both, or when a pinning layer (sub-type/file-scoped `--set`, a rule, `[files]`) set the backend at least as specifically (`br.level >= fr.level`), not strictly more specifically. Preset/config `subtypes` tables never pin. |
| CFG-33 | PC:109 | `subtype` itself cannot be set in a sub-type table | PARTLY | A `subtype` key in `[subtypes.<id>]` or `[defaults]` is accepted without error and silently overwritten by the resolved sub-type; only the `subtype:` `--set` scope refuses it (overrides.rs:236-240). |
| CFG-56 | PC:198 | `subtype`: default from the file name; not settable in `[defaults]`, `[subtypes]` or `subtype:` scopes | PARTLY | `subtype:` scope is refused; in `[defaults]`/`[subtypes]` the key is accepted and silently overwritten (see row 33). |
| CFG-100 | PC:289 | `rnm.target` `n`/`cnr`/`both`, default from the layer's name; per layer file only (`[files]` or `file:` `--set`) | PARTLY | An unscoped `--set rnm.target=...` is also accepted and applies to every layer file; only the `subtype:` scope is refused. The doc omits the unscoped form. |
| CFG-108 | PT:11 | `vanilla`: Bethesda shaders only; no `_SRGB`, no BC6H, no complex `_m`, no PBR, no complex grass | PARTLY | The `complex_grass` sub-type is still accepted and written under `vanilla` (with a `GRASS-VANILLA` warning); only the `complex_grass` set shader is unavailable. The doc's own line 37 says so. |
| TEX-4 | TT:10 | Float sources are "needed for BC6H output (HDR emissive, HDR sky)" | PARTLY | True for `pbr_emissive` only. `hdr_sky` keeps BC6H whatever the source; nothing in policy.rs gates it on a float source. |
| TEX-21 | TT:37 | `landscape_diffuse` "under `textures/landscape/`" | PARTLY | Also any path containing a whole `landscape` component (e.g. `textures/mod/landscape/`), not only `textures/landscape/` (test suggest.rs:778-800). |
| TEX-22 | TT:38 | `_n` → `normal`; `pbr_normal` in PBR set; `landscape_normal` under `textures/landscape/` | PARTLY | Same as #21: `landscape_normal` for any `landscape` path component too. |
| TEX-147 | TT:280 | When a sub-type changes this way, a format/tag you set that the new type does not allow is reset to the new type's default | PARTLY | The reset is GUI-only behaviour. On the command line the explicit value stays and the job is blocked or errors. |
| TEX-176 | VO:46 | `sdc validate` runs the same checks on existing DDS files | PARTLY | Only the checks that need no encoder input run: VAL-HEADER, VAL-ALPHA-GUARANTEE, ALPHA-254, VAL-M-*, VAL-PGPATCHER-50, VAL-GRASS-DETECT. VAL-MIP-ERROR, the exact-opaque block check, VAL-COVERAGE and VAL-BC6H-PEAK cannot run (validate/mod.rs:198, 344, 442). |
| TEX-179 | VO:51 | "per-channel PSNR" | PARTLY | One PSNR over the relevant channel set (MSE pooled across channels), not one per channel. |
| TEX-182 | VO:52 | NVTT output is held to exactly 255 | PARTLY | Validation does not special-case NVTT: an NVTT BC7 without `exact_opaque` is checked against 254 like bc7e. |
| TEX-197 | VO:75 | The model is recorded for BC1 and BC3 outputs | PARTLY | Recorded on every manifest record; only BC1 and BC3 records are compared against it. |
| SCAN-9 | Mod-Scanning.md:13 | After a scan with plugins, a texture used by a GRAS record that has the complex-grass shape is tagged `complex_grass` automatically | PARTLY | Extra conditions the doc omits: never under target `vanilla` (policy.rs:2031), only when nothing explicit (rule, `--set`, `[files]`, set shader) decided the sub-type (2026-2030), and the file must pass the 1:2 / corner / unit-normal tests (2032). The GRAS link is the slot-0 diffuse of the GRAS model's winning NIF (plugin_store.rs:356-370), not a TXST. |
| SCAN-11 | Mod-Scanning.md:15 | Renaming outputs that scanned meshes still use under their old name is flagged amber | PARTLY | The warning is skipped for PBR sets under `cs` (`if !pbr && new != old`, rename.rs:401-402) and counts only slot-0 uses of the colour member (rename.rs:475-485). |
| SCAN-24 | Mod-Scanning.md:32 | The active BSA archives, from the game INIs and the active plugins | PARTLY | Also always active, before the INI lists: every `skyrim - *.bsa` in the game Data folder (archives.rs:343-357); and the INI fallback is `<game>\Skyrim_Default.ini` when the profile/Documents INIs have no list (361-363). |
| SCAN-25 | Mod-Scanning.md:33 | The active plugins and their load order, from the profile | PARTLY | The load order is the five base masters (implicit), then `Skyrim.ccc` entries from the game folder not in plugins.txt, then the profile's `plugins.txt` `*` entries — not only the profile. |
| SCAN-34 | Mod-Scanning.md:47 | Meshes that are not Skyrim SE NIFs are counted and skipped | PARTLY | The gate is "not Skyrim LE/SE" (error text header.rs/lib.rs:308): LE (BSVER 83) NIFs are read, only other versions are counted and skipped. |
| SCAN-41 | Mod-Scanning.md:66 | Banner "PGPatcher is running, scan paused." | PARTLY | Actual text: "PGPatcher is running — scan paused." (em dash, not a comma). |
| SCAN-51 | Mod-Scanning.md:85 | Chips on each mesh: **BSA**, **game Data**, **PGPatcher**, **LE**, **Problem** | PARTLY | "BSA" and "game Data" are chips on the origin rows of the mod list, not on each mesh; mesh rows also carry a status chip and the "PGPatcher patch out of date" chip the doc omits. |
| SCAN-57 | Mod-Scanning.md:99 | **Plugin file references…** window: *Missing* (referenced but not found) and *Unused*, with **Show inert** and **Copy** | PARTLY | **Show inert** is offered only on the *Missing* list (ui/meshes.rs:631-634); the *Unused* list has no inert toggle. |
| SCAN-58 | Mod-Scanning.md:99 | Available after the scan finishes, for MO2 scans | PARTLY | The button appears whenever a scan store is open, folder scans included; after a folder scan plugins are unloaded and the window shows the "Plugins were not read in the last scan" note instead of being unavailable. |
| SCAN-79 | NVTT-Backend.md:32 | Output: `NVTT: 3.2.5 at C:\Program Files\NVIDIA Corporation\NVIDIA Texture Tools\nvcompress.exe (tested)` | PARTLY | The path is printed with forward slashes (`replace('\\', "/")`): `NVTT: 3.2.5 at C:/Program Files/NVIDIA Corporation/NVIDIA Texture Tools/nvcompress.exe (tested)`. |
| SCAN-84 | NVTT-Backend.md:57 | In `[defaults]` the backend applies only where it fits: BC7/BC6H use NVTT, BC4 and BC5 stay on rgbcx | PARTLY | Incomplete list: BC1 and BC3 also stay on rgbcx and uncompressed formats stay on `uncompressed`; any format NVTT cannot write gets its own default backend. |
| SCAN-109 | NVTT-Backend.md:98 | `VAL-BC6H-PEAK` (BC6H peak dropped below `validation.bc6h_min_peak_ratio`) suggests trying NVTT Production | PARTLY | The code and ratio check exist, but the warning text is only "BC6H keeps N% of the source peak"; nothing in code suggests NVTT for it. |
| SCAN-126 | FAQ.md:33 | Tessalith only writes to the output folder, `sdc.toml`, `sdc-project.toml` (GUI), the manifest under `<output>\_sdc\`, the scan database and its own settings | PARTLY | Also written: `%LOCALAPPDATA%\sdc\shader-cache` (crates/gui/src/model/viewport3d.rs:516-518), `scan.lock` beside scan.db (store.rs:801-824), NVTT temp folders under `%TEMP%` (nvtt.rs:197-203) and `gui.toml.bad` (settings.rs:160). |
| INST-9 | Installation.md:13 | `sdc-gui` will not start without them (all four) | PARTLY | Only `segoeui.ttf` is fatal (error dialog, exit). Symbol, Emoji and Consolas are optional: a missing one is skipped (`continue`), and without Consolas the monospace family falls back to Segoe UI. |
| INST-46 | Installation.md:120-121 | Example paths shown with backslashes (`C:\Program Files\...`, `D:\nvtt\...`) | PARTLY | The path is printed with forward slashes: `NVTT: 3.2.5 at C:/Program Files/NVIDIA Corporation/NVIDIA Texture Tools/nvcompress.exe (tested)`. |
| INST-64 | Getting-Started.md:23 | A raw roughness or metallic map, an `_rmaos` file, or a `pbr` folder each make the set PBR | PARTLY | Also glossiness and AO raw maps, and `_rfaos`, `_cnr`, `_f` members make a set PBR; and only under target `cs`. The listed triggers are correct but the list is incomplete. |
| INST-82 | Getting-Started.md:67 | `status ready`; `unresolved`, `blocked` or `error` mean not converted | PARTLY | Two more states exist and are printed by `inspect`: `packing_input` (a raw map waiting to be packed) and `normal_layer` (an RNM layer). Neither converts on its own either. |
| INST-138 | Troubleshooting.md:67 | GUI needs the four fonts and says so if one is missing | PARTLY | Only a missing `segoeui.ttf` (Segoe UI) is reported and stops the start; Segoe UI Symbol, Segoe UI Emoji and Consolas are silently skipped when absent. |
